  COMMIT 2 — Interface reservation registry + read-only live panel.             
                                                                                
  Thread-safe interface reservation registry in core/state (existing lock):     
  per-interface mode (managed/monitor) + free/reserved-by-run_id. claim/release 
  calls; claim before use, guaranteed release on finish AND on error; re-claim  
  of a held interface → clear “busy” error.                                     
  Startup reset: on dashboard start, release reservations belonging to runs     
  that are no longer alive (do NOT blindly clear reservations that may belong   
  to a genuinely-running CLI/scheduler process still using the adapter — key    
  off run liveness, not a blanket wipe).                                        
  Central interface-safety check (one reused function): refuse an interface     
  that carries the default route or active SSH, and refuse a busy interface,    
  with clear reasons. This governs monitor-mode wifi operations only — it is    
  NOT applied to normal capture on the default interface.                       
  Retrofit red.wifi_scan only to claim/release its monitor interface through    
  the registry. Do NOT modify blue.capture_tshark — it captures on the default  
  interface by design and must not be subjected to the default-route refusal.   
  If wifi_scan’s existing self-lockout guard already refuses the SSH/default    
  interface, reuse that code as the central check rather than duplicating.      
  Read-only live interfaces panel (must NEVER claim/modify an interface, and    
  must NOT run commands against an interface that is mid-capture — read cached  
  registry state + lightweight /sys/class/net, use iw dev/lsusb sparingly only  
  to label mode/USB): list present interfaces, USB-vs-built-in, driver, current 
  mode, free/busy + holding run. Live-update via existing SSE or modest poll.   
  Auth-gated; read-only (no CSRF).                                              
  Tests: claim → busy-reject → release; release on task exception; startup      
  reset frees a dead-run reservation but LEAVES a live-run reservation intact;  
  central safety check refuses default-route/SSH and busy interfaces; wifi_scan 
  claims/releases correctly; capture_tshark is unchanged and still works on     
  the default interface; panel renders state (mocked). Keep full suite +        
  contract test green.

      144 +    """True if the adapter's phy advertises monitor mode (via 
          +`iw`). Only called                                            
      145 +    for FREE Wi-Fi interfaces — never one mid-capture. Never r
          +aises."""

      150 +    args = ["iw", "phy", phy, "info"] if phy else ["iw", "dev"
          +, iface, "info"]                                              
      151 +    try:                                                      
      152 +        p = subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, text=Tru
          +e, timeout=6, check=False)                                    
      153 +        return "monitor" in ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "
          +")).lower()                                                   
      154 +    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001                         
      155 +        return False                                          
      156 +                                                              
      157 +                                                              
      158 +def monitor_interface_choices(config, registry, default: str =
          + "") -> List[dict]:                                           
      159 +    """Selectable Wi-Fi interfaces for a picker. Busy interfac
          +es are listed but                                             
      160 +    non-selectable, annotated with the holder's friendly name;
          + the default-route                                            
      161 +    / SSH interface is non-selectable (self-lockout). A busy m
          +onitor reservation                                            
      162 +    is assumed monitor-capable (never probed with iw mid-captu
          +re)."""                                                       
      163 +    reservations = registry.reservations()                    
      164 +    default_if = core_state.default_route_iface() or ""       
      165 +    ssh_if = core_state.ssh_iface()                           
      166 +                                                              
      167 +    out = []                                                  
      168 +    for iface in present_interfaces():                        
      169 +        if iface == "lo" or not is_wifi(iface):               
      170 +            continue                                          
      171 +        res = reservations.get(iface)                         
      172 +        busy = bool(res and res.get("alive"))                 
      173 +        unsafe = (iface == default_if) or (bool(ssh_if) and if
          +ace == ssh_if)                                                
      174 +        capable = True if busy else supports_monitor(iface)   
      175 +        holder = ""                                           
      176 +        if busy:                                              
      177 +            holder = res.get("label") or ("run " + str(res.get
          +("run_id", "")))                                              
      178 +        selectable = capable and not busy and not unsafe      
      179 +        if unsafe:                                            
      180 +            note = "default-route / SSH — protected"          
      181 +        elif busy:                                            
      182 +            note = "busy — in use by " + holder               
      183 +        elif not capable:                                     
      184 +            note = "no monitor mode"                          
      185 +        else:                                                 
      186 +            note = ""                                         
      187 +        out.append({                                          
      188 +            "iface": iface, "busy": busy, "holder": holder, "u
          +nsafe": unsafe,                                               
      189 +            "capable": capable, "selectable": selectable, "not
          +e": note,                                                     
      190 +            "is_default": iface == default,                   
      191 +        })                                                    
      192 +    return out                                                
      193 +                                                              
      194 +                                                              
      195 +# ------------------------------------------------------------
          +--------------- #                                             
      196 +# privileged mode switch (same scoped-sudo iw/ip approach as r
          +ed.wifi_scan)                                                 
      197 +# ------------------------------------------------------------
          +--------------- #                                             
      198 +def _priv(args: List[str]) -> List[str]:                      
      199 +    """Prefix `sudo -n` unless already root (the dashboard run
          +s unprivileged;                                               
      200 +    the scoped sudoers rule allows exactly iw/ip/airodump-ng).
          +"""                                                           
      201 +    if os.geteuid() == 0:                                     
      202 +        return list(args)                                     
      203 +    return ["sudo", "-n", *args]                              
      204 +                                                              
      205 +                                                              
      206 +def _run(args: List[str], timeout: int = 10):                 
      207 +    try:                                                      
      208 +        p = subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, text=Tru
          +e, timeout=timeout,                                           
      209 +                           check=False)                       
      210 +        return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or 
          +"")).strip()                                                  
      211 +    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001                    
      212 +        return 127, str(e)                                    
      213 +                                                              
      214 +                                                              
      215 +def set_interface_mode(iface: str, mode: str) -> tuple:       
      216 +    """Set `iface` to 'monitor' or 'managed' via iw/ip (down →
          + set type → up),                                              
      217 +    the same approach red.wifi_scan uses. Returns (ok, message
          +). Never raises.                                              
      218 +                                                              
      219 +    The CALLER must have passed the central interface_safety_c
          +heck() first — this                                           
      220 +    performs no safety gating of its own."""                  
      221 +    if mode not in ("monitor", "managed"):                    
      222 +        return (False, f"invalid mode {mode!r}")              
      223 +    _run(_priv(["ip", "link", "set", iface, "down"]))         
      224 +    rc, out = _run(_priv(["iw", "dev", iface, "set", "type", m
          +ode]))                                                        
      225 +    _run(_priv(["ip", "link", "set", iface, "up"]))           
      226 +    if rc != 0:                                               
      227 +        hint = ("needs root — the scoped sudoers rule for iw/i
          +p may be missing "                                            
      228 +                "(see deploy/sudoers.d/sentinel-wifi)") if os.
          +geteuid() != 0 \                                              
      229 +            else "iw rejected the mode change"                
      230 +        detail = f": {out}" if out else ""                    
      231 +        return (False, f"could not set {iface} to {mode} mode 
          +(rc={rc}; {hint}){detail}")                                   
      232 +    return (True, f"{iface} set to {mode} mode")

      67 +def login_csrf(client):                                        
      68 +    """Log in and return a usable CSRF token (for POST routes).
         +"""                                                            
      69 +    r = client.get("/login")                                   
      70 +    csrf = re.search(r'name="csrf" value="([0-9a-f]+)"', r.text
         +).group(1)                                                     
      71 +    client.post("/login", data={"username": "admin", "password"
         +: PW, "csrf": csrf},                                           
      72 +                follow_redirects=False)                        
      73 +    return csrf

      71 +def discovery_prefs(config) -> Dict:                           
      72 +    """Target-discovery preferences from the stored settings, w
         +ith defaults:                                                  
      73 +    {"seconds": int (1-120), "mode": "on_demand"|"background"}.
         +"""                                                            
      74 +    data = load(config)                                        
      75 +    seconds = data.get("discovery_seconds", 12)                
      76 +    try:                                                       
      77 +        seconds = max(1, min(int(seconds), 120))               
      78 +    except (TypeError, ValueError):                            
      79 +        seconds = 12                                           
      80 +    mode = data.get("target_discovery_mode", "on_demand")      
      81 +    if mode not in ("on_demand", "background"):                
      82 +        mode = "on_demand"                                     
      83 +    return {"seconds": seconds, "mode": mode}

      515 +    async def discover_wifi_route(request: Request):          
      516 +        if not current_user(request):                         
      517 +            return JSONResponse({"error": "unauthorized"}, sta
          +tus_code=401)

          +get("csrf")):

          +alidation failed"},

          +e_registry, seconds)

      542 +    @app.get("/discover/targets")                             
      543 +    def discover_targets_route(request: Request):             
      544 +        """Background-mode live target poll. In on-demand mode
          + (or read-only, or                                            
      545 +        with one adapter) it engages nothing and returns an em
          +pty list + note."""                                           
      546 +        if not current_user(request):                         
      547 +            return JSONResponse({"error": "unauthorized"}, sta
          +tus_code=401)                                                 
      548 +        prefs = settings_mod.discovery_prefs(core_config)     
      549 +        eff_mode, note = discovery_mod.effective_discovery_mod
          +e(

          +get"); }                                                      
       94 +  function setTarget(v) { var t = target(); if (t) { t.value =
          + v; t.focus(); } }                                            
       95 +  function chip(t, into) {                                    
       96 +    var b = document.createElement("button");                 
       97 +    b.type = "button"; b.className = "btn sm"; b.style.margin 
          += "2px";                                                      
       98 +    b.textContent = t.display || t.value;                     
       99 +    b.addEventListener("click", function () { setTarget(t.valu
          +e); });                                                       
      100 +    into.appendChild(b);                                      
      101 +  }                                                           
      102 +  function render(targets) {                                  
      103 +    resultsEl.innerHTML = "";                                 
      104 +    if (!targets || !targets.length) { statusEl.textContent = 
          +"No targets found."; return; }                                
      105 +    statusEl.textContent = targets.length + " found — click on
          +e to use it as the target.";                                  
      106 +    targets.forEach(function (t) { chip(t, resultsEl); });    
      107 +  }                                                           
      108 +  function post(url, data, btn) {                             
      109 +    if (btn) btn.disabled = true;                             
      110 +    statusEl.textContent = "Scanning for targets… (this can ta
          +ke a few seconds)";                                           
      111 +    resultsEl.innerHTML = "";

      113 +                 headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www
          +-form-urlencoded" },                                          
      114 +                 body: new URLSearchParams(data) })           
      115 +      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })                
      116 +      .then(function (j) {                                    
      117 +        if (j.ok) { render(j.targets); }                      
      118 +        else { statusEl.textContent = "Discovery failed: " + (
          +j.error || "unknown"); }                                      
      119 +      })                                                      
      120 +      .catch(function () { statusEl.textContent = "Discovery r
          +equest failed."; })                                           
      121 +      .finally(function () { if (btn) btn.disabled = false; })
          +;

● Both Part B commits are landed, green, and the working tree carries only the
  pre-existing unrelated changes (the PR #275 deploy/* edits, a log file, a
  transient .pyc). /health, /events, /interfaces and all existing routes are
  intact.

  Summary

  COMMIT 1 — 7d9005d Scan interface picker + mode-assignment control + busy 
  attribution
  - Busy attribution (every surface): claim() now stores a friendly label (the
    holder's workflow, e.g. Wi-Fi scan / Full Audit);
    busy_reason()/holder_label() resolve it, so the panel and pickers show "in 
    use by <task>", never a raw run_id. red.wifi_scan passes this label and now
    routes its pre-claim gate through interface_safety_check().
  - Scan launcher picker: /scan renders a <select> of present monitor-capable
    interfaces — free ones selectable, busy/protected ones shown-but-disabled
    with the holder's name, so conflicts are visible before submit. The
    selection flows into the per-run config, which wifi_scan then claims.
  - Mode-assignment control: POST /interfaces/mode (auth + CSRF,
    readonly-blocked) sets monitor/managed via the same scoped-sudo iw/ip
    approach, after interface_safety_check() refuses the default-route/SSH and
    any busy interface, briefly reserving the adapter (released in finally).
  - Settings: a default monitor interface persisted through the existing atomic
    store (env SENTINEL_WIFI_IFACE still wins, shown env-pinned).

  COMMIT 2 — 7d19d9a Smart target selection
  - On-demand (default): discover_wifi() claims a free adapter → monitor →
    bounded passive airodump sweep → selectable AP/client list → always restores
    managed + releases (abandoned/failed sweeps never hold the radio).
    discover_hosts() is a standalone nmap -sn ping sweep (does not touch
    red.recon_nmap). A "scan for targets" UI with settable duration (Settings
    default + per-use override) fills the target field; the Wi-Fi button is
    disabled with the holder's name when busy.
  - Background mode: requires 2+ adapters — effective_discovery_mode() falls
    back to on-demand with a clear note on one adapter (verified live on the
    Pi), and with 2+ BackgroundMonitor dedicates one adapter (busy,
    poll-refreshed) leaving others free. Released on shutdown.
  - Routes /discover/wifi, /discover/nmap (auth+CSRF+readonly) and the
    /discover/targets poll; strict single-adapter sequencing through the
    registry (never a double claim).

  Throughout: offline server-rendered Jinja + light inline JS, autoescaped,
  guaranteed release on error, interface_safety_check() on every interface
  operation. Works today with the single WN722N (wlan1, 8188eu) while
  SSH/default route stay on wlan0; ready for the second adapter tomorrow. Full 
  suite + contract test green: 243 passed. Stopping here.

  COMMIT 1 — Full Audit (passive chain). A “Full Audit” chained workflow        
  running the passive/safe modules only (red.recon_nmap → red.wifi_scan →       
  blue.capture_tshark → blue.esp_sensor) in sequence, feeding results forward   
  via the store scratch space + depends_on (recon’s discovered hosts/ports      
  become inputs for later steps, no retyping). Explicitly EXCLUDE any offensive 
  module from the auto-chain (offensive stays manual + gated). Uses the         
  existing orchestrator path (validate_target, readonly apply). On one adapter, 
  Wi-Fi steps sequence through the registry; a step whose interface is          
  busy/unavailable is reported as skipped with a reason, never silently         
  dropped. Adding a module to the chain is data, not code. Show chained step    
  progress in the live view. Tests: chains passive modules, feeds data forward, 
  reports skipped steps, excludes offensive. Commit.                            
                                                                                
  COMMIT 2 — Global live indicator + stop + force-release.                      
                                                                                
  Global live-scan indicator: add a persistent status element to the shared     
  base layout/template that all dashboard pages already extend (do NOT edit     
  each page/route individually) — shows any active run (workflow, target,       
  elapsed, link to live view), survives navigation, unobtrusive when idle.      
  Reads the same active-run/live-state source as the overview badge, so it      
  reflects dashboard-triggered AND scheduler-launched runs.                     
  Stop active scan: a control on the global indicator (auth + CSRF,             
  readonly-blocked) to abort the running scan — cleanly cancel the run, and     
  ensure any interface it held is released via the registry (do not leave a     
  stuck reservation). Confirm before stopping; log it.                          
  Force-release interface: a control in the interface panel (auth + CSRF,       
  readonly-blocked) to manually free a stuck interface reservation without      
  restarting the dashboard — confirm before releasing, log it; uses the         
  registry’s release.                                                           
  ESP in the hardware panel: add the ESP sensor as a present device row (port   
  /dev/ttyACM0, alive from its last heartbeat/blue.esp_sensor status) alongside 
  the Wi-Fi interfaces, so the panel is the complete “what hardware is          
  attached” view. Read-only row.                                                
  Tests: global indicator reads shared state incl. scheduler runs; stop cancels 
  a run and releases its interface; force-release frees a reservation; ESP row  
  renders from sensor status. Keep suite + contract test green. Commit.         
                                                                                
  Throughout: offline server-rendered Jinja + light inline JS; autoescape ON;   
  escape derived values; auth/CSRF/readonly respected; interface release on     
  error preserved. Don’t break /health, /events, /interfaces, existing routes,  
  auth, lockout, or the scheduler. Keep the full suite + contract test green.   
  Then STOP.

      133 +def _recon_host_filter(ctx) -> str:                           
      134 +    """Build a BPF 'host A or host B …' from hosts a prior rec
          +on step published to                                          
      135 +    the store scratch (`recon.hosts`), so a Full-Audit capture
          + focuses on the                                               
      136 +    discovered hosts without the operator retyping them. Retur
          +ns "" when no recon                                           
      137 +    hosts were forwarded, there are too many to filter cleanly
          +, or a value is not                                           
      138 +    a valid IP (never let a stray value reach the BPF)."""    
      139 +    hosts = ctx.store.get("recon.hosts") or []                
      140 +    valid = []                                                
      141 +    for h in hosts:                                           
      142 +        try:                                                  
      143 +            ipaddress.ip_address(str(h))                      
      144 +        except ValueError:                                    
      145 +            continue                                          
      146 +        valid.append(str(h))                                  
      147 +    if not valid or len(valid) > _MAX_FILTER_HOSTS:           
      148 +        return ""                                             
      149 +    return " or ".join(f"host {h}" for h in valid)

      942 +                                  should_stop=run_state.stop_r
          +equested)                                                     
      943      except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
      944          log.error("dashboard-triggered run failed: %s", e)
      945          if live:
      946              live.finalize(error=str(e))
      947      finally:
      948 +        # Safety net: free any interface this run still holds 
          +(a module's own                                               
      949 +        # finally normally does this; this guarantees no stuck
          + reservation even                                             
      950 +        # on a stop/crash).                                   
      951 +        if run_id:                                            
      952 +            try:                                              
      953 +                freed = get_interface_registry(run_config).rel
          +ease_run(run_id)                                              
      954 +                if freed:                                     
      955 +                    log.info("run %s: released interface(s) on
          + finish: %s", run_id, freed)                                  
      956 +            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001            
      957 +                log.warning("run %s: interface release failed:
          + %s", run_id, e)                                              
      958          detach_log_capture(handle)
      959          if live and not live.done:
      960              live.finalize()

● Both commits are landed, green, and the working tree carries only
  pre-existing/unrelated changes (the PR #275 deploy/* edits, a log file, and
  tracked .pyc build artifacts — no source left unstaged). All five key routes
  are present.

  Summary

  COMMIT 1 — ba24b8d Full Audit passive chain with data forwarding
  - full_audit is now the chained passive sweep red.recon_nmap → red.wifi_scan →
    blue.capture_tshark → blue.esp_sensor, run through the existing
    orchestrator path (ordered steps, validate_target, readonly,
    skip-with-reason).
  - Passive-only: every chained module is offensive=False; offensive modules are
    excluded and stay manual + gated.
  - Data forwarded via store scratch (no retyping): recon_nmap publishes
    recon.hosts/recon.open_ports; capture_tshark, when no BPF filter is set,
    focuses the capture on those hosts (bounded, IP-validated). An explicit
    filter still wins; capture_only is unchanged.
  - Single-adapter aware: the Wi-Fi step sequences through the registry;
    busy/absent hardware yields a skipped finding with a reason (never silently
    dropped). Chained progress already shows per-step in the live view. Adding a
    step is data.

  COMMIT 2 — 76f95d8 Global indicator + stop + force-release + ESP row
  - Global live indicator added once to the shared base.html, injected via
    render() (active defaults to run_state.current() on every page) — workflow,
    target, live elapsed, live-view link — reflecting dashboard- and
    scheduler-launched runs, unobtrusive when idle.
  - Stop active scan (POST /scan/stop, auth+CSRF, readonly-blocked, confirmed,
    logged): a minimal, backward-compatible should_stop predicate checked
    between steps (current step finishes, rest skip, "stopped by operator"
    recorded); _bg_run's finally release_run()s any interface the run held — no
    stuck reservation.
  - Force-release (POST /interfaces/release, auth+CSRF, readonly-blocked,
    confirmed, logged): frees a stuck reservation via the registry without a
    restart, naming the holder.
  - ESP hardware row: the interfaces page's new "Attached hardware" section
    shows a read-only ESP32-S3 row (port + alive/silent/busy + last seen) from
    blue.esp_sensor status.

  Throughout: offline server-rendered Jinja + light inline JS, autoescaped,
  auth/CSRF/readonly respected, guaranteed interface release on error,
  interface_safety_check() on every interface op. /health, /events, /interfaces,

      181 +def _parse_ssl_cert(output: str) -> dict:                     
      182 +    d = {}

      202 +            http["server"] = str(out).strip()[:200]

      207 +# ------------------------------------------------------------
          +--------------- #                                             
      208 +# full option set: presets, safe arg building, validation, hon
          +est estimator                                                 
      209 +# ------------------------------------------------------------
          +--------------- #                                             
      210 +# Presets seed an option set; the existing fast/full modes map
          + onto quick/standard.                                         
      211 +# A preset is a starting point — explicit per-field options st
          +ill override it.                                              
      212 +PRESETS = {                                                   
      213 +    "quick":    {"fast": True,  "version": False, "os": False,
          + "scripts": "",                                               
      214 +                 "ports": ("top", FAST_TOP_PORTS), "intensity"
          +: None},                                                      
      215 +    "standard": {"fast": False, "version": True,  "os": None, 
          + "scripts": "default",                                        
      216 +                 "ports": ("top", FULL_TOP_PORTS), "intensity"
          +: None},                                                      
      217 +    "thorough": {"fast": False, "version": True,  "os": None, 
          + "scripts": "default,safe,discovery",                         
      218 +                 "ports": ("top", FULL_TOP_PORTS), "intensity"
          +: 7},                                                         
      219 +    "deep":     {"fast": False, "version": True,  "os": None, 
          + "scripts": "default,safe,discovery",                         
      220 +                 "ports": ("all", None), "intensity": None},  
      221 +}                                                             
      222 +                                                              
      223 +_TECHNIQUE_FLAG = {"syn": "-sS", "connect": "-sT", "udp": "-sU
          +", "ack": "-sA"}                                              
      224 +_ROOT_TECHNIQUES = {"syn", "udp", "ack"}       # raw-socket te
          +chniques: need root                                           
      225 +_INTRUSIVE_CATEGORIES = {"vuln", "intrusive", "exploit", "brut
          +e", "dos"}                                                    
      226 +                                                              
      227 +                                                              
      228 +def _validate_plan(scan_types: List[str], privileged: bool, ex
          +plicit_os: bool) -> None:                                     
      229 +    """Reject genuinely-incompatible combos with a clear reaso
          +n (never silently)."""                                        
      230 +    picked = [s for s in scan_types if s]                     
      231 +    if len(picked) > 1:                                       
      232 +        raise ValueError("choose a single scan technique (got:
          + %s)" % ", ".join(picked))                                    
      233 +    st = picked[0] if picked else ""                          
      234 +    if st and st not in _TECHNIQUE_FLAG:                      
      235 +        raise ValueError(f"unknown scan technique: {st!r}")   
      236 +    if st in _ROOT_TECHNIQUES and not privileged:             
      237 +        raise ValueError(f"the {st} scan technique ({_TECHNIQU
          +E_FLAG[st]}) requires "                                       
      238 +                         "root/privileged mode")              
      239 +    if explicit_os and not privileged:                        
      240 +        raise ValueError("OS detection (-O) requires root/priv
          +ileged mode")                                                 
      241 +                                                              
      242 +                                                              
      243 +def _resolve_plan(config, target: str, privileged: bool) -> di
          +ct:                                                           
      244 +    """Merge the preset (or fast/full default) with explicit p
          +er-field options                                              
      245 +    into one normalized plan. Raises ValueError on an incompat
          +ible combo."""                                                
      246 +    preset = (getattr(config, "nmap_preset", "") or "").strip(
          +).lower()                                                     
      247 +    if preset in PRESETS:                                     
      248 +        base = dict(PRESETS[preset])                          
      249 +        fast = base["fast"]                                   
      250 +    else:                                                     
      251 +        fast = config.nmap_fast if config.nmap_fast is not Non
          +e else _is_large_target(target)                               
      252 +        base = {"fast": fast, "version": (not fast), "os": (No
          +ne if not fast else False),                                   
      253 +                "scripts": "", "ports": ("top", FAST_TOP_PORTS
          + if fast else FULL_TOP_PORTS),                                
      254 +                "intensity": None}                            
      255 +                                                              
      256 +    raw_st = (getattr(config, "nmap_scan_type", "") or "").str
          +ip().lower()                                                  
      257 +    scan_types = [s for s in re.split(r"[,\s]+", raw_st) if s]
      258 +    explicit_os = config.nmap_os is True                      
      259 +    _validate_plan(scan_types, privileged, explicit_os)       
      260 +    technique = _TECHNIQUE_FLAG.get(scan_types[0] if scan_type
          +s else "",                                                    
      261 +                                    "-sS" if privileged else "
          +-sT")                                                         
      262 +                                                              
      263 +    if getattr(config, "nmap_all_ports", False):              
      264 +        ports = ("all", None)                                 
      265 +    elif (getattr(config, "nmap_port_spec", "") or "").strip()
          +:                                                             
      266 +        ports = ("custom", config.nmap_port_spec.strip())     
      267 +    elif config.nmap_top_ports and config.nmap_top_ports > 0: 
      268 +        ports = ("top", config.nmap_top_ports)                
      269 +    else:                                                     
      270 +        ports = base["ports"]                                 
      271 +                                                              
      272 +    version = bool(config.nmap_version) if config.nmap_version
          + is not None else bool(base["version"])                       
      273 +    intensity = config.nmap_version_intensity                 
      274 +    if intensity is None:                                     
      275 +        intensity = base.get("intensity")                     
      276 +                                                              
      277 +    if config.nmap_os is not None:                            
      278 +        os_on = bool(config.nmap_os) and privileged           
      279 +    else:                                                     
      280 +        base_os = base["os"]                                  
      281 +        os_on = privileged if base_os is None else (bool(base_
          +os) and privileged)                                           
      282 +                                                              
      283 +    scripts = (getattr(config, "nmap_scripts", "") or "").stri
          +p() or base.get("scripts", "")                                
      284 +                                                              
      285 +    return {"fast": fast, "technique": technique,             
      286 +            "ping_only": bool(getattr(config, "nmap_ping_only"
          +, False)),                                                    
      287 +            "pn": bool(config.nmap_pn), "ports": ports, "versi
          +on": version,                                                 
      288 +            "intensity": intensity, "os": os_on, "scripts": sc
          +ripts,                                                        
      289 +            "timing": config.nmap_timing or "4", "timeout": co
          +nfig.nmap_timeout}                                            
      290 +                                                              
      291 +                                                              
      292 +def _args_from_plan(plan: dict) -> List[str]:                 
      293 +    """Build the nmap argument list from a resolved plan (list
          +, never shell=True)."""                                       
      294 +    timing = f"-T{plan['timing']}"                            
      295 +    timeout = []                                              
      296 +    if plan["timeout"] and plan["timeout"] > 0:               
      297 +        timeout = [f"--host-timeout {plan['timeout']}s"]      
      298 +                                                              
      299 +    if plan["ping_only"]:                                     
      300 +        # -sn: host discovery only; nmap ignores ports/version
          +/os/scripts here.                                             
      301 +        parts = ["-sn"]                                       
      302 +        if plan["pn"]:                                        
      303 +            parts.append("-Pn")                               
      304 +        return parts + [timing] + timeout                     
      305 +                                                              
      306 +    parts = [plan["technique"]]                               
      307 +    if plan["pn"]:                                            
      308 +        parts.append("-Pn")                                   
      309 +    kind, val = plan["ports"]                                 
      310 +    if kind == "all":                                         
      311 +        parts.append("-p-")                                   
      312 +    elif kind == "custom":                                    
      313 +        parts.append(f"-p {val}")                             
      314 +    else:                                                     
      315 +        parts.append(f"--top-ports {val}")                    
      316 +    if plan["version"]:                                       
      317 +        parts.append("-sV")                                   
      318 +        inten = plan["intensity"]                             
      319 +        if inten is not None:                                 
      320 +            try:                                              
      321 +                n = int(inten)                                
      322 +                if 0 <= n <= 9:                               
      323 +                    parts.append(f"--version-intensity {n}")  
      324 +            except (TypeError, ValueError):                   
      325 +                pass                                          
      326 +    # --version-intensity without -sV is silently omitted (han
          +dled above), not an error.                                    
      327 +    if plan["os"]:                                            
      328 +        parts.append("-O")                                    
      329 +    cats = [c.strip() for c in (plan["scripts"] or "").split("
          +,") if c.strip()]                                             
      330 +    if cats == ["default"]:                                   
      331 +        parts.append("-sC")                                   
      332 +    elif cats:                                                
      333 +        parts.append(f"--script={','.join(cats)}")            
      334 +    parts.append(timing)                                      
      335 +    return parts + timeout                                    
      336 +                                                              
      337 +                                                              
      338 +def estimate_cost(config, target: str = "") -> dict:          
      339 +    """Honest relative cost: a category (fast|moderate|slow|ve
          +ry-slow) + the named                                          
      340 +    cost drivers present + an intrusiveness flag. No false-pre
          +cision minutes — all                                          
      341 +    options stay selectable; this only informs."""            
      342 +    drivers: List[str] = []                                   
      343 +    intrusive = False                                         
      344 +    preset = (getattr(config, "nmap_preset", "") or "").strip(
          +).lower()                                                     
      345 +    st = (getattr(config, "nmap_scan_type", "") or "").lower()
      346 +    scripts = (getattr(config, "nmap_scripts", "") or "").lowe
          +r()                                                           
      347 +    all_ports = bool(getattr(config, "nmap_all_ports", False))
          + or preset == "deep"                                          
      348 +    large = _is_large_target(target)                          
      349 +                                                              
      350 +    very = slow = False                                       
      351 +    if "udp" in st:                                           
      352 +        drivers.append("UDP scan"); very = True               
      353 +    if all_ports:                                             
      354 +        drivers.append("all 65535 ports"); very = True        
      355 +    if any(c in scripts for c in _INTRUSIVE_CATEGORIES):      
      356 +        drivers.append("vuln/intrusive NSE scripts"); intrusiv
          +e = True; slow = True                                         
      357 +    if large:                                                 
      358 +        drivers.append("large subnet"); slow = True           
      359 +    inten = getattr(config, "nmap_version_intensity", None)   
      360 +    try:                                                      
      361 +        if inten is not None and int(inten) >= 7:             
      362 +            drivers.append("high version-detection intensity")
          +; slow = True                                                 
      363 +    except (TypeError, ValueError):                           
      364 +        pass                                                  
      365 +    if getattr(config, "nmap_pn", False) and large:           
      366 +        drivers.append("-Pn over a large subnet"); slow = True
      367 +                                                              
      368 +    if very:                                                  
      369 +        category = "very-slow"                                
      370 +    elif slow:                                                
      371 +        category = "slow"                                     
      372 +    else:                                                     
      373 +        fast = config.nmap_fast if config.nmap_fast is not Non
          +e else large                                                  
      374 +        category = "fast" if (preset == "quick" or fast) else 
          +"moderate"                                                    
      375 +    return {"category": category, "drivers": drivers, "intrusi
          +ve": intrusive}                                               
      376 +                                                              
      377 +

      379  class ReconNmap(Module):
      380      manifest = Manifest(

      265 +    function syncIntensity() {                                
      266 +      // --version-intensity applies only when -sV is on; grey
          + it otherwise.                                                
      267 +      var ver = document.getElementById("f_nmap_version");    
      268 +      var inten = document.getElementById("f_nmap_version_inte
          +nsity");                                                      
      269 +      if (ver && inten) inten.disabled = (ver.value !== "on");
      270 +    }                                                         
      271 +    function applyEstimate(e) {                               
      272 +      if (!e || e.error) return;                              
      273 +      var cat = document.getElementById("est-cat");           
      274 +      var drv = document.getElementById("est-drivers");       
      275 +      var intr = document.getElementById("est-intrusive");    
      276 +      if (cat) cat.textContent = e.category || "—";           
      277 +      if (drv) drv.textContent = (e.drivers && e.drivers.lengt
          +h) ? (" · " + e.drivers.join(", ")) : "";                     
      278 +      if (intr) intr.innerHTML = e.intrusive ? ' · <span style
          +="color:var(--orange)">intrusive</span>' : "";

      280 +      box.setAttribute("data-intrusive", e.intrusive ? "1" : "
          +0");                                                          
      281 +      var warn = (e.category === "slow" || e.category === "ver
          +y-slow" || e.intrusive);                                      
      282 +      box.className = "banner" + (warn ? " warn" : "");       
      283 +    }                                                         
      284 +    function refresh() {                                      
      285 +      var data = new URLSearchParams(new FormData(form));

      288 +        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-url
          +encoded" }, body: data })                                     
      289 +        .then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(applyEst
          +imate).catch(function () {});                                 
      290 +    }                                                         
      291 +    form.addEventListener("change", function () {             
      292 +      syncIntensity();

● Both phases are done and green. All work is committed locally (push is blocked
  — see below).

  Final summary

  PHASE 1 — recon capability + enriched assets

  P1C1 6230194 — enriched Asset schema + unified device-type inference + reader 
  tolerance
  - Extended Asset with optional, default-empty recon fields: ports[]
    (port/proto/state/service/product/version/extrainfo/banner), os_accuracy
    (nullable), device_type/device_confidence/device_reasoning, scripts (NSE,
    truncated-with-note), tls, http.
  - red.recon_nmap now populates them from the nmap result (banners, OS
    accuracy, nmap's osclass device type, port/host NSE, TLS/HTTP parse) —
    defensively, so missing fields never crash.
  - infer_device_type() — one coherent guess: trusts nmap's classification when
    -O matched (high confidence), else port/service/MAC-vendor heuristics; never
    two labels.
  - Backward compat verified: a pre-enrichment run renders/aggregates/loads fine
    (readers use .get()).

  P1C2 11a27c7 — full nmap option set + honest estimator + audit guard
  - Extended the existing option handling (no parallel system) into the full
    grouped set: host discovery (-sn/-Pn), port selection (top-N / all /
    custom), single-choice technique (-sS/-sT/-sU/-sA), version detection +
    intensity (emitted only with -sV, silently omitted otherwise), OS detection,
    NSE (default/categories, vuln separable), timing. Presets
    quick/standard/thorough/deep; fast/full map on and defaults reproduce the 
    previous args byte-for-byte. Full Audit stays non-deep.
  - Args built as a list; incompatible combos rejected with clear reasons (dual
    technique; root-only/-O without root) via is_available + run guards.
  - estimate_cost() — honest category (fast/moderate/slow/very-slow) + named
    drivers + intrusive flag, no fake minutes. Generated args sanity-checked
    live on localhost (bounded).

  PHASE 2 — dashboard UI

  P2C1 00f2a20 — scan options UI + live estimate
  - Presets one-click + "Advanced" expandable exposing every group with
    plain-language descriptions + time impact. Technique as radio single-select;
    root-only options disabled/flagged when unprivileged; vuln flagged
    intrusive; --version-intensity greyed unless -sV on. Live honest estimate

  GPU-friendly transform/opacity animation; respect prefers-reduced-motion; do  
  NOT animate every row of large lists (the asset table may have 60+ rows —

      472 +        return "";

          + : "";                                                        
      475 +      return (el.value || "").trim();

✻ Baked for 7m 3s · done Sunday 1 Nov, 0:48

❯ Bug: the “Privileged scan mode” checkbox in Settings does not persist.        
  Checking it and clicking “Save config” refreshes the page with the box        
  unchecked again — the setting isn’t saved or isn’t reflected. Diagnose the    
  real cause, fix it, and verify end-to-end. Scope: only this settings          
  persistence/rendering path — do NOT change scan logic, the scoped-sudo        
  mechanism, auth, or unrelated settings. Keep the full suite + contract test   
  green. Then STOP.                                                             
                                                                                
  Diagnose (find which it actually is):                                         
                                                                                
  The POST handler for the settings/config form — how does it read the          
  checkbox? Remember an unchecked HTML checkbox sends no field at all, and a    
  checked one sends its value (often "on"). If the handler expects true/false,  
  or doesn’t treat “field absent = False / field present = True”, it will       
  always store False. Check this first.                                         
  Whether the value is actually written to persistent config                    
  (data/dashboard.json or wherever runtime settings persist) on save — confirm  
  by inspecting the file after a save.                                          
  Whether the checkbox’s rendered checked-state reads from the same source that 
  was saved. If the template renders checked from a default or from an env var  
  that takes precedence over stored config, a saved true would never show.      
  Verify the env-vs-stored-config precedence doesn’t override the saved value   
  for display.                                                                  
                                                                                
  Fix whichever is the cause: correctly parse the checkbox on POST (absent →    
  False, present → True), persist it atomically via the existing                
  settings-persistence mechanism (same as other settings), and render the       
  checkbox’s checked state from the persisted value (respecting the documented  
  env/stored precedence, but a user-saved stored value must be honored and      
  shown). Ensure other existing settings on the same form still save correctly  
  (don’t regress them — note that any OTHER unchecked checkbox on the form      
  could have the same absent-field bug; fix the pattern consistently for all    
  checkboxes on the settings form).                                             
                                                                                
  Verify end-to-end: after the fix, saving with the box checked results in the  
  persisted config holding the enabled value AND the page re-rendering with the 
  box checked; saving unchecked persists disabled and renders unchecked.        
  Add/extend a test that POSTs the settings form with the checkbox on and off   
  and asserts the persisted value and the rendered checked-state both match.    
  Keep the full suite + contract test green.                                    
  Then STOP.

● Committed. Here's the diagnosis and outcome.

  Diagnosis

  I traced the full persistence/render path for the Settings "Privileged scan
  mode" checkbox and reproduced the exact browser flow (render form → POST all
  fields with the box toggled → follow redirect → inspect the re-rendered
  checkbox + the on-disk settings file):

  ┌───────────────────┬────────────────────────┬─────────────────┐
  │      Action       │     Persisted file     │ Re-rendered box │
  ├───────────────────┼────────────────────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ Save with box on  │ nmap_privileged: true  │ checked         │
  ├───────────────────┼────────────────────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ Save with box off │ nmap_privileged: false │ unchecked       │
  ├───────────────────┼────────────────────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ Save on again     │ true                   │ checked         │
  └───────────────────┴────────────────────────┴─────────────────┘

  So against the current committed code, all three suspected failure modes are
  already correct:
  1. POST parsing — handles HTML checkbox semantics correctly (unchecked = field
     absent → False; checked → True).
  2. Persistence — written atomically to the same dashboard_settings.json store
     as every other setting.
  3. Render source — the checkbox's checked state reads from the persisted
     config value; env precedence is documented (SENTINEL_NMAP_PRIVILEGED) and
     isn't set by default, so a user-saved value is honored and shown (only an
     actual env pin overrides it, in which case the box is shown disabled
     "(env-pinned)").

  The only conditions that correctly suppress it are an env pin and read-only
  mode (by design). The symptom the report describes does not reproduce against
  the current code — the most likely real-world cause is a stale deployed build
  (the fix shipped in the earlier privileged-mode commit, but the push has been
  blocked in this environment, so a running instance may predate it).

  What I changed

  - Hardened the pattern uniformly: both form checkboxes (readonly,
    nmap_privileged) now go through one explicit checkbox() parser (absent →
    False, present → True), so no checkbox on the settings form can silently
    store False. No behavior change to scan logic, the scoped-sudo mechanism,
    auth, or other settings.
  - Added an end-to-end regression test that asserts both the persisted value
    and the rendered checked-state for the on and off cases (plus a fresh GET),
    and that toggling privileged doesn't regress the other settings.

  Scope stayed within the settings persistence/render path. Full suite + 
  contract test green — 329 passed.

  ⚠ Push still blocked

  git push can't run here (HTTPS remote, no credentials/gh). main is 7 commits 
  ahead of origin/main. If the deployed dashboard was showing the bug, it's
  almost certainly running a pre-fix build — publish and restart it with:
  ! git push origin main
  then redeploy/restart the sentinel-dashboard service. Committed locally;
  nothing lost.
